and digital marketing services.

     Non-IFRS Measure                                                      非國際財務報告準則計量
     To supplement our consolidated financial statements, which are        為補充我們根據國際財務報告準則列報的綜合財務
     presented in accordance with IFRSs, we also use adjusted net          報表，我們還採用經調整淨利潤（非國際財務報告
     profit (non-IFRS measure) (as defined below) as an additional         準則計量）
                                                                               （定義見下文）作為額外財務計量，其並
     financial measure, which is not required by, or presented in          非國際財務報告準則的要求，或按照國際財務報告
     accordance with IFRSs.                                                準則所呈列。


     We define “adjusted net profit (non-IFRS measure)” as loss for the    我們將「經調整淨利潤（非國際財務報告準則計量）」
     year, adding back changes in carrying amount of the redemption        定義為年內虧損，並加回贖回負債賬面值變動及上
     liabilities and listing expenses. The carrying amount of the          市開支。贖回負債的賬面值屬非現金性質。上市開
     redemption liabilities are non-cash in nature. The listing expenses   支為與全球發售有關的開支。
     are expenses related to our global offering.


     The adjusted net profit (non-IFRS measure) can provide useful         經調整淨利潤（非國際財務報告準則計量）通過與其
     information to investors in understanding and evaluating our          幫助我們管理層的相同方式，為投資者在了解及評
     consolidated results of operations in the same manner as it helps     估我們的綜合經營業績時提供有用資料。
     our management.

     financial measure.                                                    賬。

     TREASURY POLICIES AND FOREIGN                                           庫務政策及外匯風險
     EXCHANGE EXPOSURE
     We have adopted prudent treasury policies for both long-term            我們已就長期和短期現金管理採取審慎的庫務政
     and short-term cash management. While the primary objective             策。雖然我們的長期投資主要目標為投資或收購能
     of our long-term investments is to invest in or acquire businesses      與我們業務互補的業務，但我們亦著重短期投資機
     that are complementary to our business, we focus on short-term          會，據此以高於現行銀行存款利率的收益率賺取財
     investment opportunities, which could generate finance income           務收入，並重視資本保值。我們的短期投資一般為
     at a yield higher than current deposit bank interest rates, with        商業銀行或其他持牌金融機構所發行流動性較高、
     an emphasis on capital preservation. Our short-term investments         風險較低的金融工具。我們根據個別情況作出投資
     usually go to relatively liquid and low-risk financial instruments      決策，投資期限取決於管理層對現金流需求的預
     issued by either commercial banks or other licensed financial           測。
     institutions. We make our investment decisions on a case-by-
     case basis, and the investment duration is dependent on the
     management’s projection of our cash flow requirement.

     and accountability.

     in Appendix C1 to the Listing Rules as the basis of the Company’s    治常規的基準。
     corporate governance practices.

Code provision C.2.1 of the CG Code stipulates that the role of      企業管治守則守則條文第C.2.1條規定，主席及行

     Since Mr. Zhang is the key reason for the Group’s development              由於張先生為本集團發展的關鍵及彼於任何情況下
     and he will not undermine the Group’s interests in any way under           均不會以任何方式損害本集團的利益，董事會認
     any circumstances, the Board considers that vesting the roles              為，主席及行政總裁的角色由同一人（即張先生）擔
     of chairman and chief executive officer in the same person, Mr.            任不會對本集團的利益構成任何潛在損害，反之對
     Zhang, would not create any potential harm to the interest of the          本集團的管理有利。
     Group and it is, on the contrary, beneficial to the management of
     the Group.

     and balances the power and authority of Mr. Zhang, as both the             先生的權力及職權。董事會目前由兩名執行董事
     chairman and chief executive officer of the Group. The Board               （包括張先生）、兩名非執行董事及三名獨立非執行
     currently comprises two executive directors (including Mr. Zhang),         董事組成，故其組成的獨立程度相當大。
     two non-executive directors and three independent non-executive
     directors and therefore has a fairly strong independence element

     Dividend policy                                                        股息政策
     As advised by our Cayman Islands legal advisor, under Cayman           誠如開曼群島法律顧問所告知，開曼群島法律規
     Islands law, a position of accumulated losses does not necessarily     定，累計虧損不一定會限制本公司從利潤或股份溢
     restrict our Company from declaring and paying dividends to            價賬中向股東宣派及派付股息，前提是董事會根據
     our Shareholders out of either our profit or our share premium         本公司的財務狀況及利潤，有理據認為宣派及派付
     account, provided this appears to the Board to be justified by the     股息不會導致緊隨建議支付股息的日期後，本公司
     financial conditions and the profits of the Company and would          無法支付於日常業務過程中到期應付的債務。
     not result in our Company being unable to pay its debts as they
     fall due in the ordinary course of business immediately following
     the date on which the dividend is proposed to be paid.


     As we are a holding company incorporated under the laws of             由於我們是一家根據開曼群島法律註冊成立的控股
     the Cayman Islands, the payment and amount of any future               公司，任何未來股息的派付及金額亦將視乎我們自
     dividends will also depend on the availability of dividends received   附屬公司收取的可用股息。我們派付的任何股息
     from our subsidiaries. Any dividends we pay will be determined         將由董事會考慮各種因素後全權酌情決定，包括實
     at the absolute discretion of our Board, taking into account           際及預期的經營業績、現金流量及財務狀況、一般
     factors, including our actual and expected results of operations,      業務狀況及業務戰略、預期營運資金需求以及未來
     cash flow and financial position, general business conditions and      擴展計劃、法律、監管及其他合約限制，以及董事
     business strategies, expected working capital requirements and         會認為適當的其他因素。我們的股東可在股東大會
     future expansion plans, legal, regulatory and other contractual        上批准任何股息宣派，但不得超過董事會建議的金
     restrictions, and other factors that our Board deems to be             額。於整個往績記錄期，本公司並無支付或宣派任
     appropriate. Our Shareholders may approve, in a general meeting,       何股息。
     any declaration of dividends, which must not exceed the amount
     recommended by our Board. Throughout the Track Record Period,
     the Company did not pay or declare any dividend.


     Currently, the Company intends to retain most, if not all, of our      目前，本公司擬保留大部分（如非全部）可用資金及
     available funds and any future earnings to fund the development        任何未來盈利，為我們業務的發展與成長提供資
     and growth of our business. As a result, we have not yet adopted       金。因此，我們尚未就未來股息採納正式的股息政
     a formal dividend policy or a fixed dividend distribution ratio with   策或固定的股息分配比例。

RISK MANAGEMENT AND INTERNAL                                              風險管理及內部控制
CONTROLS
The Board acknowledges its responsibility for the risk management         董事會承認其須對風險管理及內部監控系統負責，
and internal control systems and reviewing their effectiveness.           並有責任檢討該等制度的有效性。該等系統旨在管
Such systems are designed to manage rather than eliminate                 理而非消除未能達成業務目標的風險，而且只能就
the risk of failure to achieve business objectives, and can only          不會有重大的失實陳述或損失作出合理而非絕對的
provide reasonable and not absolute assurance against material            保證。
misstatement or loss.


The Board has the overall responsibility for evaluating and               董事會整體負責評估及釐定本公司達成策略目標時
determining the nature and extent of the risks it is willing to take      所願意接納的風險性質及程度，並確保設立及維持
in achieving the Company’s strategic objectives, and establishing         合適及有效的風險管理及內部監控系統。
and maintaining appropriate and effective risk management and
internal control systems.


The Audit Committee assists the Board in leading the management           審核委員會協助董事會領導管理層及監督彼等對風
and overseeing their design, implementation and monitoring of             險管理及內部控制系統的設計、實施、監察及有效
the risk management and internal control systems.                         性。


The Company’s risk management and internal control systems                本公司已制定其風險管理及內部控制系統，其具備
have been developed with the following features and processes:            以下特點及流程


(a)   The processes used to identify, evaluate and manage                 (a)   本集團用於識別、評估及管理重大風險的程
      significant risks by the Group are summarised as follows:                 序概述如下：


      Risk Identification: Identifies risks that may potentially affect         風險識別：識別可能對本集團的業務及營運
      the Group’s business and operations.                                      造成潛在影響之風險。


      Risk Assessment: Assesses the risks identified by using the               風險評估：利用管理層制定之評估標準評估
      assessment criteria developed by the management; and                      所識別的風險；及考慮對業務之影響及後果
      considers the impact and consequence on the business and                  以及發生之可能性。
      the likelihood of their occurrence.


      Risk Response: Prioritises the risks by comparing the results             風險應對：透過比較風險評估的結果確定風
      of the risk assessment; and determines the risk management                險的優先順序；及釐定風險管理策略及內部
      strategies and internal control processes to prevent, avoid or            控制程序以預防、避免或減輕該等風險。
      mitigate the risks.

Board of Directors’ Statement                                          董事會聲明
The Board of Directors of the Group assumes full responsibility        本集團董事會對集團的環境、社會及管治策略及匯
for the Group’s environmental, social and governance strategy          報承擔全部責任，負責評估及釐定集團有關環境、
and reporting, is responsible for evaluating and determining           社會及管治的風險，並確保集團設立合適及有效的
the Group’s environmental, social and governance risks, and            環境、社會及管治風險管理及內部監控系統。
ensuring that the Group has established appropriate and effective
environmental, social and governance risk management and
internal control systems.


We build smart medical bridges to empower doctors, improve             我們構築智慧醫療橋樑，賦能醫生，提升醫療整體
the overall level of medical care, solve the problem of uneven         水平，破解資源不均難題，為患者鋪設健康之路，
resources, pave the HealthyWay for patients, and provide the           通過構建一體化的健康管理網絡，為大眾提供高
public with high-quality and accessible medical services by building   質量且觸手可及的醫療服務。我們的發展機遇來自
an integrated health management network. Our opportunities             於人們日益增長的健康需求以及對高質量生活的追
for development come from people’s growing health needs                求，為此本集團格外關注企業的可持續發展，建立
and their pursuit of a high-quality life. To this end, the Group       健全ESG管治體系，由董事會領導並參與ESG重大
pays particular attention to corporate sustainable development,        事宜的審議與決策，包括識別與評估ESG風險、制
establishes and improves the ESG governance system, and has the        定ESG戰略與方針、確定ESG事項的優先級、建立
Board of Directors lead and participate in the review and decision-    管理政策與計劃、審批和審視ESG目標管理，並批
making of major ESG matters, including identifying and assessing       准每年度的ESG報告。
ESG risks, formulating ESG strategies and policies, prioritizing ESG
matters, establishing management policies and plans, approving
and reviewing ESG target management, and approving annual
ESG reports.


Based on the external environment and the Group’s development          基於外部環境和本集團發展戰略，報告期內本集團

personnel should be contacted immediately via OA and

should be responded to within 4 hours. Crisis complaints
need to be reported to the person in charge of the highest
level of management, who will promptly make a judgment
on the crisis, coordinate and mobilize the resources from
relevant departments, and formulate a crisis management
plan.

                                             環境、社會及管治報告



    Case: The Pharmaceutical Corporate Cooperation Department held a special anti-corruption propaganda
    conference on “Integrity and Self-Discipline, Sticking to the Bottom Line”.
    案例：藥企合作事業部開展「廉潔自律 · 堅守底線」反腐倡廉專題宣講大會

    On 10 April 2024, the Group’s Pharmaceutical Corporate Cooperation Department held a special anti-
    corruption propaganda conference on “Integrity and Self-Discipline, Sticking to the Bottom Line”,
    and all members of the department participated in the conference. The conference pointed out that
    anti-corruption is a solid foundation for the progress of society and enterprises, and required that the
    department’s next focus to further improve the system construction, vigorously promote the development
    of an honest administration culture, and strengthen accountability. The conference required that the
    department’s new marketing personnel sign an anti-bribery commitment letter, which was of great
    significance for further enhancing the integrity awareness of department personnel.
    2024年4月10日，集團藥企合作事業部開展「廉潔自律 · 堅守底線」
                                      反腐倡廉專題宣講大會，部門全體成員

Case: DIY aromatherapy activities for International Women’s Day
案例：「三八」婦女節香薰DIY活動

On 7 March 2024, the Group’s headquarters organized an aromatherapy DIY activity to enhance
communication and interaction among women and demonstrate the Group’s care for female employees.
At the event, we shared instructional videos on aromatherapy, introduced the methods and processes of
aromatherapy, and provided a manual on aromatherapy procedures, enabling employees to experience a
unique DIY aromatherapy journey.
2024年3月7日，為增進女性之間的交流和互動，關愛女性員工，本集團總部舉辦了一場香薰DIY活動。
活動為員工們播放香薰製作視頻、介紹香薰製作方法和流程、提供香薰步驟手冊，讓員工們體驗一次獨
特的DIY香薰製作之旅。

services and health and medical services. The Company mainly           過三個分部經營其業務：(i)企業服務及數字營銷服

marketing services; (ii) the health and medical services segment       多種線上及線下渠道從事健康服務及產品的銷售，
is engaged in the provision of health and medical services which       例如代表客戶探訪病人服務及銷售營養補充劑。本
mainly consists of medical support services and value-based            公司主要在國內市場開展業務。

      On March 29, 2022, the State Council promulgated the                於2022年3月29日，國務院頒佈《國務院關於修改
      Decision of the State Council on Revising and Repealing Certain     和廢止部分行政法規的決定》，當中修訂了當時的
      Administrative Regulations (《 國務院關於修改和廢止部分行                         《外商投資電信企業管理規定》

      政法規的決定》), which amended the then Regulations for                    企業規定》」），有關修訂於2022年5月1日起生效。
      the Administration of Foreign-Invested Telecommunications           根據《新外商投資電信企業規定》，外商投資者獲准
      Enterprises (《 外商投資電信企業管理規定》) (the “New FITE                        持有須取得ICP許可證方可經營業務的實體最多（但
      Regulations”) with the amendments taking effect on May 1,           不得多於）50%股權，前提是該外商投資者須首先
      2022. Under the New FITE Regulations, foreign investors are         符合資格規定，包括註冊資本及《新外商投資電信
      permitted to hold up to 50% (but no more) equity interest in        企業規定》所要求的其他規定或證明（「增值電信資
      entities requiring an ICP License to operate, provided that the     格要求」）。然而，《新外商投資電信企業規定》刪除
      foreign investor satisfies qualification requirements, including    了有關外商投資者須在海外經營增值電信業務方面
      registered capital and other requirements or proof required under   擁有良好往績記錄及經驗的規定。
      the new FITE Regulations (“VAT Qualification Requirements”).
      However, the New FITE Regulations removed the requirements
      that the foreign investor would need to have a good track record
      and experience in operating VAT businesses overseas.

Pricing Basis                                                          定價基準
The transactions contemplated under the Wellness Content               科普內容合作協議項下擬進行交易的定價乃根據
Cooperation Agreements are priced based on the CPM model,              CPM模式釐定，據此，我們就每千頁點閱率向百
whereby we charge Baidu Netcom a certain amount of service             度網訊收取一定金額的服務費。該服務費金額乃根
fee for every 1,000 page views. Such amount of service fee is          據百度對所有內容提供商提供的實時報價釐定，該
determined based on the real-time quotation provided by Baidu to       報價不時波動。於往績記錄期，我們收取的服務費
all content providers, which fluctuates from time to time. During      金額介乎每千頁點閱率人民幣10.67元至每千頁點
the Track Record Period, such amount of service fee we charged         閱率人民幣27.04元。CPM模式是業內常用的定價
ranged from RMB10.67 every 1,000 page views to RMB27.04                模式。於CPM模式下，每千頁點閱率的單價由百
every 1,000 page views. CPM model is a common pricing model            度根據大數據分析單獨計算及決定，並且會不時波
adopted in the industry. Under CPM model, the unit price for           動。百度向所有可資比較內容提供商（包括本公司
every 1,000 page views is calculated and decided solely by Baidu       及百度的其他獨立第三方）提供標準單價。因此，
based on its big data analysis, which fluctuates from time to          我們的董事不斷由公共資源及資訊中獲取並審查類
time. Baidu offers standard unit price to all comparable content       似交易中的現行市場價格，並將其與百度向其獨立
providers, including our Company and other Baidu’s independent         第三方及我們提供的波動價格進行比較。
third parties. Therefore, our Directors constantly obtain and review
market prevailing price in similar transactions from public resource
and information, and compare those with the fluctuating price
offered by Baidu to its independent third parties and us.

     doubt on the Group’s ability to continue as a going concern.        大疑慮。倘我們認為存在重大不確定性，則

     required to draw attention in our auditor’s report to the           表中的相關披露。假若有關的披露不足，則

     opinion. Our conclusions are based on the audit evidence            而，未來事項或情況可能導致         貴集團終止
     obtained up to the date of our auditor’s report. However,           持續經營。

1   GENERAL INFORMATION                                             1   一般資料
    HealthyWay Inc. (the “Company”) was incorporated in                 健康之路股份有限公司（「本公司」）於2014年
    the Cayman Islands on 18 November 2014 as an exempted               11月18日在開曼群島根據開曼群島公司法第
    company with limited liability under the Companies Law,             22章（1961年第3號法例）
                                                                                      （ 經綜合及修訂）註
    Cap22 (Law 3 of 1961, as consolidated and revised) of the           冊成立為一家獲豁免有限責任公司。
    Cayman Islands.


    Since 30 December 2024 (the “Listing Date”), the                    自2024年12月30日（「上市日期」）起，本公司
    Company’s shares has been listed on the Main Board of               股份於香港聯合交易所有限公司（「聯交所」）
    the Stock Exchange of Hong Kong Limited (the “Stock                 主板上市。

                making the judgements about carrying values of assets

                       Significant increases in credit risk                                  信貸風險大幅上升

                       In assessing whether the credit risk of a financial                   評估金融工具的信貸風險自初始
                       instrument has increased significantly since                          確認以來有否大幅上升時，本集
                       initial recognition, the Group compares the risk                      團會比較於報告日期及於初始確
                       of default occurring on the financial instrument                      認日期所評估的金融工具發生違
                       assessed at the reporting date with that assessed                     約的風險。在進行此項重新評估

           (l)   Inventories                                                      (l)   存貨

                 in the rendering of services.

                 realisable value.                                                      賬。

                 formula and comprises all costs of purchase, costs of                  所有採購成本、加工成本及使存貨處於
                 conversion and other costs incurred in bringing the                    當前地點及狀況所產生的其他成本。
                 inventories to their present location and condition.

                 ordinary course of business less the estimated costs of                價減去完成及銷售所需的估計成本後的
                 completion and the estimated costs necessary to make                   金額。
                 the sale.

                 those inventories is recognised as an expense in the                   關收入獲確認的期間內確認為開支。任

    (q) Redemption liability                                                 (q) 贖回負債
        The Group’s obligation to redeem its shares for cash                   倘發生本集團及持有人均無法控制的事

                 comprehensive income.

                 receivable on the taxable income or loss for the year                  稅虧損的估計應付或應收稅款以及對過
                 and any adjustments to the tax payable or receivable                   往年度應付或應收稅款作出的任何調
                 in respect of previous years. The amount of current tax                整。即期應付或應收稅款金額為預期繳
                 payable or receivable is the best estimate of the tax                  納或應收稅款的最佳估計，反映與所得
                 amount expected to be paid or received that reflects                   稅相關的任何不確定性，其使用報告日
                 any uncertainty related to income taxes It is measured                 期頒佈或實質頒佈的稅率計量。即期稅

                 the reporting date. Current tax also includes any tax

          estimated reliably, the obligation is disclosed as a                   任披露為或然負債，但經濟利益流出的

      3    ACCOUNTING JUDGEMENTS AND                                       3   會計判斷及估計
           ESTIMATES
           Estimates and judgements are continually evaluated and are          估計及判斷會持續進行評估，並根據過往經
           based on historical experience and other factors, including         驗及其他因素而作出，包括對未來事件作出
           expectations of future events that are believed to be               認為在有關情況下屬合理的預期。
           reasonable under the circumstances.


           Key sources of estimation uncertainty in the preparation of         編製財務報表的估計不確定因素主要來源如
           the financial statements are as follows:                            下：


           (i)   Expected credit loss for trade and other                      (i)   貿易及其他應收款項的預期信貸虧
                 receivables                                                         損
                 The Group estimates impairment losses for trade                     本集團使用預期信貸虧損模型估計貿易
                 and other receivables by using expected credit loss                 及其他應收款項的減值虧損。預期信貸
                 models. Expected credit loss are estimated based on                 虧損乃基於本集團的過往信貸虧損經驗
                 the Group’s historical credit loss experience, adjusted             進行估計，並就於報告日期債務人的特
                 for factors that are specific to the debtors and an                 定因素及對當前及預計一般經濟狀況的
                 assessment of both the current and forecast general                 評估進行調整。
                 economic conditions at the reporting date.

         (i)      Pursuant to the rules and regulations of the Cayman             (i)     根據開曼群島及英屬維爾京群島的規

   rate as disclosed below. The growth rates used do not                     現率為稅前並反映與相關行業、現金產生單

25 REDEMPTION LIABILITY                                            25 贖回負債
   The Group issued financial instruments to investors for             本集團於上市前向投資者發行金融工具作融
   financing purpose before listing.                                   資用途。

   (e)   Capital management                                           (e)   資本管理
         The Group’s primary objectives when managing                       本集團管理資本的主要目標為保障本集
         capital are to safeguard the Group’s ability to                    團持續經營的能力，將產品及服務與風
         continue as a going concern, so that it can continue               險水平作相應計價，以合理的成本取得
         to provide returns for shareholders and benefits for               融資，向股東持續提供回報，為其他利
         other stakeholders, by pricing products and services               益相關者提供利益。
         commensurately with the level of risk and by securing
         access to finance at a reasonable cost.


         The Group actively and regularly reviews and manages               本集團積極及定期審視及管理其資本結
         its capital structure to maintain a balance between                構，在較高借款水平可能帶來較高股東
         the higher shareholder returns that might be possible              回報及穩健資本狀況帶來的優勢與保障
         with higher levels of borrowings and the advantages                之間取得平衡，亦因應經濟狀況轉變調
         and security afforded by a sound capital position, and             整資本結構。
         makes adjustments to the capital structure in light of
         changes in economic conditions.


         Neither the Company nor any of its subsidiaries are                本公司或其任何附屬公司並無受外界施
         subject to externally imposed capital requirements.                加的資本要求所限。


28 FINANCIAL RISK MANAGEMENT                                        28 財務風險管理及金融工具公允價
   AND FAIR VALUES OF FINANCIAL                                        值
   INSTRUMENTS
   Exposure to credit, liquidity and interest rate risks arise in     本集團於日常業務過程中產生信貸風險、流
   the normal course of the Group’s business. The Group               動資金風險及利率風險。於2024年及2023年
   does not have significant financial instruments that are           12月31日，本集團並無以外幣（即與交易有
   denominated in foreign currencies, i.e. a currency other           關的業務的功能貨幣以外的貨幣）計值的重大
   than the functional currency of the operations to which the        金融工具，因此並無面臨重大外幣風險。
   transactions relate as at 31 December 2024 and 2023, and

                 and contract assets.

                 representing low credit risk.

                (i)   Financial assets and liabilities measured at                      (i)   按公允價值計量的金融資產及
                      fair value                                                              負債

                      Fair value hierarchy                                                    公允價值層級

                      IFRS 13, Fair value measurement, requires the                           國際財務報告準則第13號，公允
                      fair value of the Group’s financial instruments                         價值計量規定本集團金融工具的
                      measured at the end of the reporting period                             公允價值，該等工具於報告期末
                      on a recurring basis, categorised into the three-                       以經常性基準計量，並按照國際
                      level fair value hierarchy as defined in IFRS 13.                       財務報告準則第13號分類為三個
                      The level into which a fair value measurement                           公允價值層級。將公允價值計量
                      is classified is determined with reference to the                       分類的等級乃經參考以下估值方
                      observability and significance of the inputs used                       法所用輸入數據的可觀察性及重
                      in the valuation technique as follows:                                  要性後釐定：


                      –   Level 1 valuations:   Fair value measured using only Level 1 inputs i.e. unadjusted quoted prices in
                                                active markets for identical assets or liabilities at the measurement date.
                      –   第一層級估值：               僅使用第一級輸入數據（即於計量日期相同資產或負債於活躍市場的未經調
                                                整報價）計量的公允價值。


                      –   Level 2 valuations:   Fair value measured using Level 2 inputs i.e. observable inputs which fail to
                                                meet Level 1, and not using significant unobservable inputs. Unobservable
                                                inputs are inputs for which market data are not available.
                      –   第二層級估值：               使用第二層級輸入數據（即未能達致第一層級的可觀察輸入數據）且並非使用
                                                重要的不可觀察輸入數據計量的公允價值。不可觀察輸入數據為無市場數據
                                                的輸入數據。


                      –   Level 3 valuations:   Fair value measured using significant unobservable inputs.
                      –   第三層級估值：               使用重要的不可觀察輸入數據計量的公允價值。
